- **lead-to-payment**: Use when a new lead arrives (email, Telegram message, or website form) and needs to move through the full pipeline: qualify the lead, generate an estimate, get owner approval, create a payment link, draft a customer email for owner review, and track payment. - **Lead-知识讲解动画**: 把一个知识选题或一句想法，做成发光线描式的知识讲解动画（MP4，无配音）：发光片名卡，开头 5 秒大字金句抓人、底部单行字幕（按内容在 IBM Plex 与思源宋体之间选字体）讲故事，画面一句一变，发光线描、粒子、体积光、真实感 UI 卡片、出处小字层层叠加，配音乐和必要音效；每次交付同时附带分平台标题与发布文案文档、横版 4K 与竖版 4K 两张代码绘制封面。流程依次是：选题角度与字幕稿、视觉系统与代表帧、时间轴、逐句分镜、确定性引擎实现、对照风格指标审片打分、混音、成片自检、文案与封面。适用于心理学、社会现象、物理宇宙、生物医学、数学概率、经济、历史人物、诺奖与科研成果、书籍观点等知识讲解。用户说“做个知识动画”“科普动画”“讲解视频”“做成发光线描风格的科普动画”“把这个知识点做成视频”“解释一下 XX 做成动画”，或给出一个选题、一句想法希望变成讲解视频时使用。已有口播视频需要剪辑包装的，交给 Lead-自媒体口播剪辑。 ([AzenYes/leaderai-media-toolkit](https://github.com/AzenYes/leaderai-media-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/lead-knowledge-animation) · ★207)
- **Lead-自媒体口播剪辑**: 把用户上传的自录口播视频直接做成可发布成片：①粗剪（删口误、重复起句、重说、无意义等待；有文案时严格按文案顺序保留最完整的一遍，无文案时按口播剪辑技巧判断）②用代码制作与文案匹配的高级动态 B-roll 讲解画面（Remotion/React/SVG/WebGL，AE 作品集级 Motion，人物缩成圆环画中画——横版在右下角、竖版在上部一角，无缝转场、配合程序化音效）③按 video-rough-cut 规范加居中单行字幕（竖版字幕上移到距底部约 1/3 处，避开平台底部信息区）并逐行检查断句 ④全盘自检后交付。用户说“口播剪辑”“剪口播”“帮我把这段口播剪成片”“口播加 B-roll/讲解动画/字幕”“粗剪+包装”，或上传一段口播视频（可附文案）要求出成片时使用。 ([AzenYes/leaderai-media-toolkit](https://github.com/AzenYes/leaderai-media-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/lead-media-edit) · ★207)

SKIP: defining values and culture from scratch -&gt; org/culture-define (creates the culture; this skill audits an existing one); trust/conflict diagnosis of one team -&gt; lead/team-health (Lencioni pyramid, not company-wide culture). - **lead/delegation-matrix**: Delegation analysis: decides what to delegate, to whom, and with which authority level, producing a delegation matrix with check-in cadence per task. - **lead/feedback-give**: Structured feedback with Radical Candor (care personally + challenge directly): specific, immediate, behavioural — situation, behaviour, impact and follow-up actions. - **leadbay_daily_check_in**: Morning DISCOVERY workflow — new leads from the lens wishlist. Trigger on "show me leads", "what's new today", "let's prospect", "run my check-in", "my morning check-in", "I do this every day", "every morning". Recurrence language always means this prompt. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_daily_check_in))
- **leadbay_extend_my_lens**: Add more leads to the current lens on demand — for users whose appetite exceeds the standard daily fill. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_extend_my_lens))
- **leadbay_followup_check_in**: Follow-up check-in: surface KNOWN leads from the Monitor view needing re-engagement. - **leadbay_getting_started**: Guided first-run walkthrough — four clicks that actually use Leadbay: check the account, pull today's leads, draft a first email to the top one, then reveal who to send it to. Use when the user is new or asks to be SHOWN how Leadbay works ("walk me through Leadbay", "I'm new", "how do I use this", "give me a tour"). Don't use it for orientation prose with no clicking — that's leadbay_prospecting_overview. - **leadbay_import_file**: Import a user-supplied CSV/file into Leadbay through five phases with evidence gates — scan, derive, resolve identities, preserve & commit, then optionally qualify and report. - **leadbay_log_outreach**: Log outreach (an email I sent, a call I made, a meeting I had) on a specific lead. Captures verification so the SDR pipeline trusts the entry. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_log_outreach))
- **leadbay_new_leads**: Guided net-new lead delivery — turn a described need ("gyms around Dallas that would buy our flooring") into ICP-perfect NEW companies with qualification evidence and the right contact, via leadbay_find_new_leads. - **leadbay_plan_tour_in_city**: Use whenever the user names a city they'll be in and asks who to see — "I'm in SF next Tuesday, who's worth meeting?", "I'm going to Berlin — who should I visit?", "plan my &lt;city&gt; tour".
